</h2><h2 data-text-none="" data-text="head-two" data-wf--head-2--variant="middle-lg-dark" class="u-head u-head_two w-variant-eee1df5d-fe3b-09cf-7f9a-6bc01baea8ee">is a composition.</h2></div><div class="content_composition-heading_max is-mobile"><h2 data-text-none="" data-text="head-two" data-wf--head-2--variant="middle-lg" class="u-head u-head_two w-variant-a56ecc1e-8c13-d3a9-2361-9dd741b15976">A home
is a composition.</h2></div></div><div class="content_composition-text is-awards"><div id="w-node-_8bbe558c-a5a1-fffb-9d83-a484e624e501-9735b123" class="content_composition-text_wrapper is-awards"><p data-text-none="" data-text="body" data-wf--body--variant="base" class="u-body u-body_body">In a well-composed interior, nothing
is accidental. 1.09793 1.15641 5.9828e-07 1.15647L5.68682e-07 1.83362H0.813344C1.61958 1.69469 2.29568 1.33748 2.78952 0.744795L5.18666 0.744795L5.18666 15.2552H2.78952C2.29568 14.6625 1.61959 14.3053 0.813344 14.1664H2.95986e-08L0 14.8435C1.09794 14.8436 1.88209 15.229 2.35266 16Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></div></div><h2 data-text-none="" data-text="head-two" data-wf--head-2--variant="base" class="u-head u-head_two">Questions clients
often ask</h2></div><div class="content_faq-questions"><div id="w-node-de785bd8-e662-a0a1-4b70-c812d726c490-9735b123" class="content_faq-questions-inline"><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">Do you only create the design project, or do you manage the renovation end to end?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>We support the project at every stage — from the initial concept to a fully furnished and completed space.</p><p>Our services are divided into separate blocks, which can be commissioned together or individually: interior design, engineering design, budgeting, design supervision, project management and procurement.</p><p>The construction work itself is carried out by a licensed general contractor, whom we select for you through a tender process. At the same time, we manage and oversee the entire process: the tender, cost estimate, schedule, quality control and compliance with the design project.</p><p>So yes, we offer an end-to-end renovation management service — but not through our own in-house construction team. Instead, we manage the full implementation process on your behalf.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">What is included in the design project, and what will I receive?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>The design project consists of three main sections.</p><p><strong>Design concept<br/></strong>We study your aesthetic preferences, collect references, create a moodboard and develop the overall visual concept for the future interior.</p><p><strong>3D visualisation<br/></strong>We create a three-dimensional model of the space, develop all materials and textures, work through daytime and evening lighting scenarios, and prepare photorealistic 4K renders with post-production.</p><p><strong>Architectural documentation<br/></strong>This is a complete set of technical drawings, including: measurement plans, demolition plans, plans for new structures and partition details, furniture layout plans with dimensions and markings, furniture specifications, floor and ceiling drawings and details, lighting plans and lighting specifications, socket and switch layouts, engineering layouts for air conditioning, ventilation, heating and plumbing, elevations and sections for all rooms, custom furniture drawings and a finishes schedule.</p><p>You receive a fully approved set of project documentation that can be used to prepare an accurate cost estimate, build a work schedule and start the renovation without additional revisions.</p><p>Engineering design — including heating, plumbing, boiler room, ventilation, air conditioning, electrics and lighting — is a separate service block with its own drawings and equipment specifications.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">How much does the design project cost, and what affects the price?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>A tailored approach is at the heart of how we work. We do not use templates: every property is unique, so the cost is calculated individually based on the brief, budget and lifestyle of the client.</p><p>The price depends on the size of the property, the scope of required sections, the type of space — residential or commercial — the interior style and the number of custom solutions.</p><p>The current condition of the property is also important. A major renovation with layout changes and replacement of engineering systems requires a very different design and technical approach compared to a new apartment that only needs light cosmetic updates. Naturally, these projects will also differ in cost.</p><p>Tell us about your property, and we will quickly prepare an estimate tailored to your project.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">How is the renovation estimate prepared, and what does it include?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>The estimate is prepared based on the approved interior design project, engineering design, finishes schedule, material and equipment specifications, and information about the property.</p><p>It is prepared in two languages — Russian and Spanish — and includes work across all sections, materials, equipment and additional costs.</p><p>We then form a pool of general contractors, check their licences and run a tender. Each contractor completes the same estimate template with their own pricing. We compare the proposals, identify mistakes, missing works and unjustified costs, select the contractor and agree the final estimate with you.</p><p>As a result, you receive a transparent and verified calculation — not a rough “by eye” price.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div class="faq-more"><div style="height:0px" class="faq-more_wrapper"><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="last" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-variant-eec10381-ba6b-7251-0f36-209a95416420 w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">What happens if the final cost of works exceeds the estimate?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>The final estimate is fixed after the tender and included in the contract with the general contractor. This becomes the final cost of works and can only be changed by an additional agreement between the parties.</p><p>As part of project management, we maintain a consolidated budget and payment schedule, and monitor the progress of works against the approved timeline to prevent overspending.</p><p>Cost increases are usually possible in two cases: if you make changes to the project or scope of work after approval, or if hidden site conditions are discovered — for example, issues with existing structures — that could not have been assessed in advance.</p><p>Any such changes are agreed and formalised separately.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="last" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-variant-eec10381-ba6b-7251-0f36-209a95416420 w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">What are the stages of work — from first enquiry to completion?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>Everything starts with an enquiry and a brief: we get to know each other, discuss the task, budget and your preferences.</p><p>The work then follows three main stages.</p><h3><strong>1. Design</strong></h3><p>Interior design project: concept, 3D visualisation and technical documentation.<br/>Engineering design.</p><h3><strong>2. Budgeting</strong></h3><p>Cost estimate and tender: we prepare the estimate, check contractor licences and select the contractor through a tender process.<br/>Development of a detailed work schedule and contract with the contractor.<br/>Preparation of the procurement budget: materials, furniture, lighting and décor.</p><h3><strong>3. Implementation</strong></h3><p>Project launch, design supervision and project management throughout the entire construction process.<br/>Procurement and logistics: purchasing, timeline control, delivery coordination, acceptance on site and installation.<br/>Final handover of the completed property.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="last" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-variant-eec10381-ba6b-7251-0f36-209a95416420 w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">How long does the design project take, and how long does the renovation take?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>Timelines, like costs, are always individual. They depend on the size of the property, the scope of work and the current condition of the space. A major renovation and a light cosmetic update move at very different speeds.</p><p>On average, the design stage takes from 1 to 4 months, while construction takes from 6 to 12 months.</p><p>The exact timeline is included in the detailed work schedule, which we use to manage and control the entire process.</p><p>Tell us about your property, and we will prepare a realistic schedule for your project.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">What is design supervision, and why do I need it?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>Design supervision means that the designer supports the construction process to make sure the final result matches the approved project.</p><p>As part of supervision, the designer visits the site 1–2 times a week, works directly with the construction team, checks the progress against the project every week, updates and adjusts the project when needed, prepares additional documentation, approves paint samples, material samples and custom-made items, and helps resolve complex issues that could not have been predicted in advance.</p><p>Without design supervision, there is a high risk that the construction team will deviate from the project or make incorrect decisions on site — which can lead to wasted time and money.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">What is project management, and why is it necessary?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>Project management is the coordination of the entire process — from design to final handover — so that you do not have to deal with organisational issues and can receive a completed result on time and within budget.</p><p>Already at the design stage, we take over communication with neighbours, the building management company and subcontractors, coordinating everything needed for future implementation.</p><p>During construction, we organise the tender between general contractors, check estimates from the general contractor and subcontractors, prepare a consolidated budget and payment schedule, develop the full project timeline and monitor compliance with it, provide monthly reports with photo and video updates, communicate with the client’s lawyer, launch the site, set the rules for work on site, introduce all participants to their roles and handle all organisational issues throughout the project.</p><p>Without dedicated management, the process can easily get out of control: deadlines shift, the budget grows and transparency is lost.</p><p>Project management removes these risks and keeps the entire process under control — from the first approvals to the final handover.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">How many rounds of revisions are included?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>Our goal is to design and deliver a project that you are truly happy with — while keeping the process on schedule and within budget.</p><p>That is why we carefully develop every decision together with you and make revisions during the approval process until we reach the final version.</p><p>The work is divided into phases, and each phase is approved before we move on to the next one. Once a phase has been signed off — for example, the layout — it is considered final.</p><p>Changes to an already approved phase are possible, but they are billed separately on an hourly basis, as they require reworking related decisions and documentation.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">What warranties do you provide for the renovation?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>The warranty for construction works is provided by the licensed general contractor. Its terms and duration are fixed in the contract, which we help prepare and review.</p><p>Our contractors are confident in the quality of their work and provide a full warranty:</p><p><strong>1 year</strong> — for defects in finishing works;<br/><strong>3 years</strong> — for everything that affects living comfort, including humidity, insulation and engineering systems;<br/><strong>10 years</strong> — for the structural safety of the property.</p><p>On our side, we are responsible for the quality of the project documentation, the verification of estimates and contractors, and for ensuring that the final result matches the approved project through design supervision.</p><p>This way, you are protected at every level: the design, the implementation and the long-term reliability of the property.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">Do I need permits for the renovation, and do you handle them?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>In Spain, renovation and construction works usually require a permit — <em>licencia de obras</em>. The type of permit depends on the scope of work, as well as the requirements of the specific municipality and building.</p><p>We take care of this process for you. Our licensed architects prepare the required documentation and organise the permit process.</p><p>You do not need to deal with the bureaucracy yourself — we handle it on your behalf.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">Do you purchase materials and furniture, or is that my responsibility?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>We can take care of all purchases for you through our procurement service.</p><p>You receive not only 10–20% discounts from stores that work with designers, but also a fully managed process where logistics and bureaucracy are taken off your hands.</p><p>We select a pool of suppliers, find the best prices and conditions, agree materials, furniture, lighting and décor with you, prepare documentation for custom-made items and create a procurement budget.</p><p>After that, we handle everything: placing orders, organising delivery, accepting items on site, quality control, claims, replacements and returns in case of defects, as well as delivery and installation schedules.</p><p>You do not need to deal with invoices, deadlines or supplier negotiations — we manage the process end to end.</p></div></div></div></nav></div><div data-delay="650" data-hover="false" data-wf--faq-item--variant="main" data-w-id="2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef658fe" class="faq-item w-dropdown"><div class="faq-item_trigger w-dropdown-toggle"><h3 data-text="head-three" class="u-head u-head_four is-faq">Can we work together remotely if I live in another country or city?</h3><div class="faq-item_trigger-arrow"><div class="u-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none"><path d="M4.5 12.5L10 7.5L15.5 12.5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></div></div><nav class="faq-item_content w-dropdown-list"><div class="faq-item_content-inline"><div id="w-node-_2ba42e11-d83f-56fd-479a-50b46ef65907-6ef658fe" class="faq-item_content-max"><div class="u-rich w-richtext"><p>Yes. The interior design project and engineering design can be developed remotely: concept approval, visualisations and documentation are all coordinated online, and all documentation is prepared in Russian and Spanish.</p><p>Implementation on site — including design supervision and project management — takes place locally in Barcelona and Catalonia. The designer regularly visits the site, so you do not need to be permanently present in the city.</p><p>To keep you fully informed, you will have a personal Notion workspace with all projects, documents, estimates, reports and site photos updated in real time.</p><p>You can follow the progress online from anywhere in the world — without constant calls or status messages.</p></div></div></div></nav></div></div><button data-w-id="b56152e0-4677-3fc1-bada-d857ad7e53a7" data-button-swap="swap-trigger" class="u-btn"><span data-text="show-swap" class="span">show null}}setup(t,e){if("undefined"==typeof SplitText)return null;if("chars"===e.type){var a=new SplitText(t,{type:"chars",charsClass:"char-wrap"});return t.splitInstance=a,gsap.set(a.chars,{opacity:0,yPercent:20,willChange:"transform,opacity",force3D:!0,backfaceVisibility:"hidden",display:"inline-block"}),a.chars}var n=new SplitText(t,{type:"words",wordsClass:"word-wrap"});return <script>document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",()=>{let t=document.querySelectorAll("[data-text-counter]");if(!("IntersectionObserver"in window)){t.forEach(t=>startCounter(t));return}let e=new IntersectionObserver((t,e)=>{t.forEach(t=>{if(t.isIntersecting){let n=t.target;startCounter(n),e.unobserve(n)}})},{threshold:.2});t.forEach(t=>{t.textContent="0",e.observe(t)})});function startCounter(t){let e=parseFloat(t.getAttribute("data-text-counter"))||0,n=performance.now();function r(o){var a;let u=Math.min((o-n)/1e3,1),i=1-(1-(a=u))*(1-a);t.textContent=Math.round(0+(e-0)*i),u<1?requestAnimationFrame(r):t.textContent=e}requestAnimationFrame(r)}</script>




<script>/**

 * Apartments section scroll animations

 * Source: webflow.faaafb9f.7a590ca3d7e105c7.js → u-section.is-apart

 *

 * Animates ALL CMS slides (querySelectorAll), not only the first.

 * Swiper unlocks when scrub reaches “fully visible”;

 * on reverse scroll below that point → slideTo(0) + disable.

 */

!(function () {

  "use strict";




  var SECTION = ".u-section.is-apart";

  var EASE = "power2.inOut";




  var UNLOCK_AT = {

    desktop: 1.5 / 3.12,

    mobile: 1.0 / 2.5,

  };




  var unlocked = false;




  function qa(root, sel) {

    return Array.prototype.slice.call(root.querySelectorAll(sel));

  }




  function q(root, sel) {

    return root.querySelector(sel);

  }




  function unlockSwiper(section) {

    if (unlocked) return;

    unlocked = true;

    section.dispatchEvent(

      new CustomEvent("apart:ready", {

        bubbles: true,

        detail: { section: section },

      })

    );

    if (window.DataSlider && typeof window.DataSlider.init === "function") {

      window.DataSlider.init(section);

    }

  }




  function lockSwiper(section) {

    if (!unlocked) return;

    unlocked = false;

    section.dispatchEvent(

      new CustomEvent("apart:lock", {

        bubbles: true,

        detail: { section: section },

      })

    );

    if (window.DataSlider && typeof window.DataSlider.lock === "function") {

      window.DataSlider.lock(section);

    } else if (window.DataSlider && typeof window.DataSlider.destroy === "function") {

      window.DataSlider.destroy(section);

    }

  }




  function onScrubProgress(section, threshold, progress) {

    if (progress >= threshold) unlockSwiper(section);

    else lockSwiper(section);

  }




  /** t-e350b67b — chess height bars */

  function buildIntroHeights(section) {

    var left = qa(section, ".scroll-chess-item.is-left");

    var right = qa(section, ".scroll-chess-item.is-right");

    if (!left.length && !right.length) return null;




    var tl = gsap.timeline({

      scrollTrigger: {

        trigger: section,

        start: "top bottom",

        end: "top 35%",

        scrub: 0.8,

      },

    });




    if (left.length) {

      tl.fromTo(left, { height: "65svh" }, { height: "0svh", duration: 1, ease: EASE }, 0);

    }

    if (right.length) {

      tl.fromTo(right, { height: "25svh" }, { height: "0svh", duration: 1, ease: EASE }, 0);

    }




    return tl;

  }




  /** t-dbf72b33 — desktop, all CMS slides */

  function buildDesktop(section) {

    var sliders = qa(section, ".content_apatments-slider");

    var backsFirst = qa(section, ".content_apatments-back.is-first");

    var backsSecond = qa(section, ".content_apatments-back.is-second");

    var numbers = qa(section, ".content_apatments-slide-number");

    var tools = qa(section, ".swiper-tools");

    var overlays = qa(section, ".content_apatments-slider--overlay");

    var footerAnims = qa(section, ".apartments_footer.is-pc.is-anim");

    var bttnSlides = qa(section, ".apartments_footer_bttn");

    var bttnOpacity = qa(section, ".apartments_footer.is-pc.is-pc-main");

    var heads = qa(section, ".apartments_main_head");

    var advantages = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantages");




    var tl = gsap.timeline({

      scrollTrigger: {

        trigger: section,

        start: "top -50%",

        end: "bottom bottom",

        scrub: 0.8,

        onUpdate: function (self) {

          onScrubProgress(section, UNLOCK_AT.desktop, self.progress);

        },

      },

    });




    if (backsSecond.length) {

      tl.fromTo(backsSecond, { scale: 0.65 }, { scale: 1.5, duration: 3, ease: EASE }, 0);

    }

    if (backsFirst.length) {

      tl.fromTo(backsFirst, { scale: 0.65 }, { scale: 1.25, duration: 3, ease: EASE }, 0.1);

    }

    if (sliders.length) {

      tl.fromTo(sliders, { scale: 0.65 }, { scale: 1, duration: 3, ease: EASE }, 0.2);

    }




    if (heads.length) {

      tl.fromTo(

        heads,

        { x: "2.2222222222222223rem", y: "-120%" },

        { x: "0rem", y: "0%", duration: 1, ease: EASE },

        0.5

      );

    }

    if (advantages.length) {

      tl.fromTo(

        advantages,

        { x: "-2.22222rem", y: "-85%" },

        { x: "0rem", y: "0%", duration: 1, ease: EASE },

        0.5

      );

    }




    if (numbers.length) {

      tl.fromTo(numbers, { opacity: 0 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.001, ease: EASE }, 1);

    }

    if (tools.length) {

      tl.fromTo(tools, { opacity: 0 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.001, ease: EASE }, 1);

    }

    if (bttnSlides.length) {

      tl.fromTo(bttnSlides, { y: "-3rem" }, { y: "0rem", duration: 2, ease: EASE }, 1);

    }

    if (footerAnims.length) {

      tl.to(footerAnims, { height: 0, duration: 0.001, ease: EASE }, 1);

    }

    if (bttnOpacity.length) {

      tl.fromTo(bttnOpacity, { opacity: 0 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.001, ease: EASE }, 1);

    }

    if (overlays.length) {

      tl.fromTo(overlays, { opacity: 0 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.001, ease: "none" }, 1.5);

    }

    if (tools.length) {

      tl.to(tools, { opacity: 0, duration: 0.12, ease: EASE }, 3);

    }




    return tl;

  }




  /** t-e769e757 — mobile, all CMS slides */

  function buildMobile(section) {

    var sliders = qa(section, ".content_apatments-slider");

    var slides = qa(section, ".content_apatments-slide");

    var heads = qa(section, ".apartments_main_head");

    var advantages = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantages");

    var advOne = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantage.is-one");

    var advTwo = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantage.is-two");

    var advThree = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantage.is-three");

    var tagTwo = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantage-tag.is-relative.is-two");

    var tagThree = qa(section, ".apartments_main_advantage-tag.is-relative.is-three");

    var captions = qa(section, ".caption-mobile");

    var headContainers = qa(section, ".apartments_main_head-container");




    var tl = gsap.timeline({

      scrollTrigger: {

        trigger: section,

        start: "top -50%",

        end: "bottom bottom",

        scrub: 0.8,

        onUpdate: function (self) {

          onScrubProgress(section, UNLOCK_AT.mobile, self.progress);

        },

      },

    });




    if (sliders.length) {

      tl.fromTo(

        sliders,

        { width: "95%", height: "35%", x: "2.5%", y: "55%" },

        { width: "100%", height: "100%", x: "0%", y: "0%", duration: 2.5, ease: EASE },

        0

      );

    }

    if (slides.length) {

      tl.fromTo(slides, { y: "-9%" }, { y: "0%", duration: 2.5, ease: EASE }, 0);

    }




    if (heads.length) {

      tl.fromTo(heads, { x: "50%" }, { x: "0%", duration: 0.35, ease: EASE }, 0.5);

    }

    if (advantages.length) {

      tl.fromTo(

        advantages,

        { x: "50%", y: "-4rem" },

        { x: "0%", y: "0rem", duration: 0.35, ease: EASE },

        0.5

      );

    }




    [advOne, advTwo, advThree].forEach(function (els) {

      if (!els.length) return;

      tl.fromTo(els, { x: "-50%" }, { x: "0%", duration: 0.35, ease: EASE }, 0.5);

    });




    [tagTwo, tagThree].forEach(function (els) {

      if (!els.length) return;

      tl.fromTo(els, { x: "50%" }, { x: "0%", duration: 0.35, ease: EASE }, 0.5);

    });




    if (captions.length) {

      tl.fromTo(captions, { x: "-50%" }, { x: "0%", duration: 0.35, ease: EASE }, 0.5);

    }

    if (headContainers.length) {

      tl.fromTo(headContainers, { x: "-50%" }, { x: "0%", duration: 0.35, ease: EASE }, 0.5);

    }




    return tl;

  }




  function init() {

    if (typeof gsap === "undefined" || typeof ScrollTrigger === "undefined") {

      console.warn("[apart] GSAP + ScrollTrigger required");

      return;

    }




    gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);




    var section = document.querySelector(SECTION);

    if (!section) return;




    unlocked = false;




    buildIntroHeights(section);




    if (window.innerWidth < 768) {

      buildMobile(section);

    } else {

      buildDesktop(section);

    }

  }




  if (document.readyState === "loading") {

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", init);

  } else {

    init();

  }




  window.addEventListener("load", function () {

    if (window.ScrollTrigger) ScrollTrigger.refresh();

  });

})();

</script>




<script>/**

 * data-slider / Swiper helper

 * - Apart section (.u-section.is-apart): no 5s timer, unlocks on `apart:ready`

 * - Manual swipe disabled (allowTouchMove: false)

 * - Other sliders: still unlock via [data-slider="trigger"] visibility (no delay)

 */

!(function () {

  "use strict";




  var DEBUG = false;

  var SEL = {

    swiper: '[data-slider="swiper"]',

    wrapper: '[data-slider="swiper-wrapper"]',

    slide: '[data-slider="swiper-slide"]',

    navigation: '[data-slider="navigation"]',

    trigger: '[data-slider="trigger"]',

    arrowLeft: '[data-slider="arrow-left"]',

    arrowRight: '[data-slider="arrow-right"]',

    scrollbar: '[data-slider="scrollbar"]',

  };




  var swiperByEl = new WeakMap();

  var timers = new WeakMap();

  var initialized = new WeakSet();

  var pending = new WeakSet();

  var observed = new WeakSet();

  var io = null;




  function log() {

    if (!DEBUG) return;

    console.log.apply(console, ["[data-slider]"].concat(Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments)));

  }




  function qs(root, sel) {

    return (root || document).querySelector(sel);

  }




  function qsa(root, sel) {

    return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel));

  }




  function merge(a, b) {

    var out = {};

    var k;

    for (k in a) if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(a, k)) out[k] = a[k];

    for (k in b) {

      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(b, k)) continue;

      if (b[k] && typeof b[k] === "object" && !Array.isArray(b[k]) && out[k] && typeof out[k] === "object") {

        out[k] = merge(out[k], b[k]);

      } else {

        out[k] = b[k];

      }

    }

    return out;

  }




  function findRoot(from) {

    var el = from;

    if (el.matches && el.matches(SEL.swiper)) el = el.parentElement;

    while (el && el !== document.documentElement) {

      if (qs(el, SEL.swiper) && el.contains(from)) return el;

      el = el.parentElement;

    }

    return null;

  }




  function getSwiperEl(root) {

    return qs(root, SEL.swiper);

  }




  function isApartRoot(root) {

    return !!(root && root.closest && root.closest(".u-section.is-apart")) ||

      !!(root && root.matches && root.matches(".u-section.is-apart"));

  }




  function decorate(root) {

    var swiperEl = getSwiperEl(root);

    var wrapper = qs(root, SEL.wrapper);

    var nav = qs(root, SEL.navigation);

    if (swiperEl) swiperEl.classList.add("swiper");

    if (wrapper) wrapper.classList.add("swiper-wrapper");

    if (nav) nav.classList.add("swiper-pagination");

    qsa(root, SEL.slide).forEach(function (slide) {

      slide.classList.add("swiper-slide");

    });

  }




  function setEnabled(root, enabled) {

    root.classList.toggle("is-slider-disabled", !enabled);

    root.classList.toggle("is-slider-enabled", enabled);

    var swiperEl = getSwiperEl(root);

    if (swiperEl) swiperEl.setAttribute("aria-disabled", enabled ? "false" : "true");

  }




  function readOptions(root) {

    var nodes = [root, getSwiperEl(root)];

    var i;

    var raw;

    for (i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {

      if (!nodes[i]) continue;

      raw = nodes[i].getAttribute("data-slider-options");

      if (!raw) continue;

      try {

        return JSON.parse(raw);

      } catch (err) {

        console.warn("[data-slider] Invalid data-slider-options JSON", err);

      }

    }

    return {};

  }




  function buildOptions(root, slideCount) {

    var user = readOptions(root);

    var canLoop = slideCount > 1;

    var nav = qs(root, SEL.navigation);

    var opts = merge(

      {

        slidesPerView: 1,

        spaceBetween: 0,

        speed: 600,

        grabCursor: false,

        allowTouchMove: false,

        simulateTouch: false,

        loop: canLoop,

        loopAdditionalSlides: canLoop ? 2 : 0,

        autoplay: {

          delay: 5000,

          disableOnInteraction: false,

          pauseOnMouseEnter: false,

        },

      },

      user

    );




    // Force behaviour for apartments / all data-sliders

    opts.allowTouchMove = false;

    opts.simulateTouch = false;

    opts.grabCursor = false;

    opts.autoplay = {

      delay: 5000,

      disableOnInteraction: false,

      pauseOnMouseEnter: false,

    };




    if (slideCount <= 1) opts.loop = false;




    if (nav) {

      opts.pagination = merge(

        { el: nav, clickable: true, type: "bullets" },

        user.pagination || {}

      );

    }




    opts.navigation = {

      prevEl: qs(root, SEL.arrowLeft),

      nextEl: qs(root, SEL.arrowRight),

    };




    opts.scrollbar = {

      el: qs(root, SEL.scrollbar),

      draggable: false,

      hide: false,

    };




    opts.on = merge(opts.on || {}, {

      init: function (swiper) {

        root.dispatchEvent(

          new CustomEvent("data-slider:init", {

            bubbles: true,

            detail: { root: root, swiper: swiper },

          })

        );

      },

      slideChange: function (swiper) {

        root.dispatchEvent(

          new CustomEvent("data-slider:change", {

            bubbles: true,

            detail: {

              root: root,

              swiper: swiper,

              activeIndex: swiper.activeIndex,

              realIndex: swiper.realIndex,

            },

          })

        );

      },

    });




    return opts;

  }




  function createSwiper(root, swiperEl, opts) {

    try {

      return new Swiper(swiperEl, opts);

    } catch (err) {

      console.warn("[data-slider] Init with loop failed, retrying without loop", err);

      opts.loop = false;

      opts.loopAdditionalSlides = 0;

      return new Swiper(swiperEl, opts);

    }

  }




  function initSlider(root) {

    if (!root) return null;

    if (initialized.has(root)) {

      var existing = getSwiperEl(root);

      return existing && swiperByEl.get(existing) || null;

    }




    if (typeof Swiper === "undefined") {

      console.error("[data-slider] Swiper library is not loaded");

      return null;

    }




    var swiperEl = getSwiperEl(root);

    if (!swiperEl) {

      console.warn('[data-slider] [data-slider="swiper"] not found in', root);

      return null;

    }

    if (!qs(root, SEL.wrapper)) {

      console.warn('[data-slider] [data-slider="swiper-wrapper"] not found in', root);

      return null;

    }




    var slides = qsa(root, SEL.slide);

    if (!slides.length) {

      console.warn('[data-slider] [data-slider="swiper-slide"] not found in', root);

      return null;

    }




    decorate(root);

    setEnabled(root, true);




    var opts = buildOptions(root, slides.length);

    var swiper = createSwiper(root, swiperEl, opts);




    if (swiper.autoplay && typeof swiper.autoplay.start === "function") {

      swiper.autoplay.start();

    }




    swiperByEl.set(swiperEl, swiper);

    initialized.add(root);

    pending.add(root);

    log("initialized", root, slides.length, "slides");




    root.dispatchEvent(

      new CustomEvent("data-slider:ready", {

        bubbles: true,

        detail: { root: root, swiper: swiper },

      })

    );




    return swiper;

  }




  function lockSlider(root) {

    if (!root) return;

    if (root.matches && root.matches(SEL.swiper)) {

      root = findRoot(root) || root.parentElement;

    }




    var swiperEl = getSwiperEl(root);

    var instance = swiperEl ? swiperByEl.get(swiperEl) : null;




    if (instance) {

      if (instance.autoplay && typeof instance.autoplay.stop === "function") {

        instance.autoplay.stop();

      }

      try {

        if (instance.params && instance.params.loop && typeof instance.slideToLoop === "function") {

          instance.slideToLoop(0, 0);

        } else {

          instance.slideTo(0, 0);

        }

      } catch (err) {

        try {

          instance.slideTo(0, 0);

        } catch (e2) {}

      }

      instance.destroy(true, true);

      swiperByEl.delete(swiperEl);

    }




    initialized.delete(root);

    pending.delete(root);

    setEnabled(root, false);

    log("locked", root);

  }




  function scheduleInit(root) {

    if (!root || initialized.has(root) || pending.has(root)) return;

    // Apart section is unlocked only by apart:ready / DataSlider.init

    if (isApartRoot(root)) {

      log("apart root waits for apart:ready", root);

      return;

    }

    pending.add(root);

    initSlider(root);

  }




  function onTriggerVisible(trigger) {

    var root = findRoot(trigger);

    if (!root) {

      console.warn("[data-slider] Slider root not found for trigger", trigger);

      return;

    }

    if (initialized.has(root) || pending.has(root)) return;

    if (isApartRoot(root)) return;

    scheduleInit(root);

  }




  function observeSize(el) {

    var rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();

    if (rect.width === 0 && rect.height === 0 && el.parentElement) {

      log("trigger has zero size, observing parent", el);

      return el.parentElement;

    }

    return el;

  }




  function bindTrigger(trigger) {

    if (!io || observed.has(trigger)) return;

    var target = observeSize(trigger);

    io.observe(target);

    observed.add(trigger);




    var rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();

    if (

      rect.bottom > 0 &&

      rect.top < window.innerHeight &&

      rect.right > 0 &&

      rect.left < window.innerWidth

    ) {

      onTriggerVisible(trigger);

    }

  }




  function collectTriggers() {

    var triggers = qsa(document, SEL.trigger);

    if (triggers.length) return triggers;




    log('no [data-slider="trigger"] found, using slider root as trigger');

    var roots = [];

    qsa(document, SEL.swiper).forEach(function (el) {

      var root = findRoot(el) || el.parentElement;

      if (root && roots.indexOf(root) === -1) roots.push(root);

    });

    return roots;

  }




  function registerAll() {

    var roots = [];

    qsa(document, SEL.swiper).forEach(function (el) {

      var root = findRoot(el) || el.parentElement;

      if (!root || roots.indexOf(root) !== -1) return;

      roots.push(root);

      decorate(root);

      if (!initialized.has(root)) setEnabled(root, false);

    });

    log("registered sliders", roots.length);

  }




  function bindAll() {

    registerAll();




    var triggers = collectTriggers();

    if (!("IntersectionObserver" in window)) {

      triggers.forEach(onTriggerVisible);

      return;

    }




    if (!io) {

      io = new IntersectionObserver(

        function (entries) {

          entries.forEach(function (entry) {

            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;

            collectTriggers().forEach(function (trigger) {

              if (observeSize(trigger) === entry.target || trigger === entry.target) {

                onTriggerVisible(trigger);

              }

            });

          });

        },

        { root: null, rootMargin: "0px 0px -5% 0px", threshold: 0.01 }

      );

    }




    triggers.forEach(bindTrigger);

    log("bound triggers", triggers.length);

  }




  function onApartReady(event) {

    var section = (event && event.detail && event.detail.section) || event.target;

    if (!section) return;

    var root =

      (section.matches && section.matches(".u-section.is-apart") && section) ||

      section.closest(".u-section.is-apart") ||

      section;

    initSlider(root);

  }




  function onApartLock(event) {

    var section = (event && event.detail && event.detail.section) || event.target;

    if (!section) return;

    var root =

      (section.matches && section.matches(".u-section.is-apart") && section) ||

      section.closest(".u-section.is-apart") ||

      section;

    lockSlider(root);

  }




  document.addEventListener("apart:ready", onApartReady);

  document.addEventListener("apart:lock", onApartLock);




  function boot() {

    bindAll();

  }




  if (document.readyState === "loading") {

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);

  } else {

    boot();

  }




  window.addEventListener("load", bindAll);




  if ("MutationObserver" in window) {

    var moTimer = null;

    new MutationObserver(function () {

      if (moTimer) window.clearTimeout(moTimer);

      moTimer = window.setTimeout(bindAll, 100);

    }).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  }




  window.DataSlider = {

    init: function (target) {

      if (typeof target === "string") target = qs(document, target);

      if (!target) return null;

      if (target.matches && target.matches(SEL.swiper)) {

        target = findRoot(target) || target.parentElement;

      }

      return initSlider(target);

    },

    get: function (target) {

      if (typeof target === "string") target = qs(document, target);

      if (!target) return null;

      if (target.matches && target.matches(SEL.swiper)) {

        return swiperByEl.get(target) || null;

      }

      var swiperEl = getSwiperEl(target);

      return swiperEl && swiperByEl.get(swiperEl) || null;

    },

    destroy: function (target) {

      if (typeof target === "string") target = qs(document, target);

      if (!target) return;

      lockSlider(target);

    },

    lock: function (target) {

      if (typeof target === "string") target = qs(document, target);

      if (!target) return;

      lockSlider(target);

    },

    debug: function (value) {

      DEBUG = value !== false;

    },

  };

})();

</script></body></html>

take this as a reference and create a totel ui for this project
